*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Engine-Transfer-Bench（ETB），一个用于在不同文档编译引擎（pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst、pandoc）之间进行系统化选择和评估的基准框架，包含1,784个开放文档、四项任务和跨操作系统（macOS、Ubuntu、Windows）的宿主标签结果。研究发现Tectonic在稳定性上表现最佳，经典TeX Live系列引擎的成功率受发行版策略影响波动12-20个百分点，而702个可移植LaTeX文档在所有引擎上均可100%成功编译，延迟成为主要选择因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一个统一的框架来帮助用户在多个文档编译引擎（pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst、pandoc等）之间进行选择和迁移，导致用户难以客观比较各引擎的可靠性、性能和兼容性。

Method: 构建ETB基准，包含1,784个开放文档，设计四项任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性、失败分析），使用固定（pinned）测试框架，并在GitHub Actions上跨macOS、Ubuntu和Windows三个操作系统进行大规模编译测试（每个宿主4,211次编译），同时引入S_pdf文本一致性度量并进行50对验证。

Result: Tectonic成功率稳定在96.3-97.2%（波动0.9个百分点以内），而经典TeX Live系列引擎在不同发行版策略下波动12-20个百分点；702个可移植LaTeX文档在所有引擎上100%成功，失败集中在107个引擎特定模板中，失败原因主要是架构层面的字体、布局和资源问题而非缺失包；S_pdf文本一致性度量在50对验证中达到94%的精确率。

Conclusion: ETB提供了首个系统化的引擎选择基准框架，表明对于可移植文档延迟是主要选择因素，而失败主要源于架构性差异而非包缺失。作者发布了ETB、ETB-Porta、推荐器和可移植性门控工具，以及公共跨操作系统测试框架，作为共享基础设施供社区使用。

Abstract: There is no shared framework for selecting among document compilation engines (pdfLaTeX, XeLaTeX, LuaLaTeX, Tectonic, Typst, and pandoc PDF backends). We present Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB): 1,784 open documents, four tasks covering reliability, latency, text consistency, and failures, a pinned harness, and host-tagged multi-OS results. On GitHub Actions (N=4,211 compiles per host across macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows), Tectonic success is stable within 0.9 percentage points (96.3-97.2%), whereas classic TeX Live-style engines vary by 12-20 percentage points according to distribution policy (Ubuntu apt, MiKTeX auto-install, or macOS BasicTeX). On 702 portable LaTeX documents, the tested engines succeed at 100%, making latency the primary selection factor; failures concentrate in 107 engine-specific templates. Within ETB, failures are architectural, involving fonts, layout, and assets, rather than missing packages on a provisioned host. A 50-pair validation of the S_pdf text-consistency metric achieves 94% precision for real content divergence. We release ETB, ETB-Porta, a recommender and portability gate, and a public cross-OS harness as shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [16] [One Gate Is Not Enough: Composing Stateful Pre-Action Controls for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18360)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究Agentic AI系统中多重前置控制门（权限、资源、证据）之间的"修复诱导控制耦合"问题，提出remediate-and-regate协议恢复每次操作的声音性，并通过有限模型检验证明两种修复算子不可交换（顺序成为控制面语义的一部分），同时发现受治理证据缓冲区存在状态级相同问题。实验显示三个发布引擎组合在30个种子下CH1-CH5满足决策规则，CH6仅在较大工作流下满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统在一次操作执行前同时受到多重控制（权限门、资源门、证据门）的约束，这些控制可以准入、降级或修复操作。一个控制执行的修复可能改变另一个控制所评估的操作、证据或上下文，从而使其先前的判断失效。这种"控制耦合"问题未被正式建模，导致多控制环境下每次操作的合理性（soundness）缺乏保证。

Method: 1. 形式化定义修复诱导的控制耦合；2. 提出remediate-and-regate协议，在给定有界、幂等设定及其假设下恢复每次操作的合理性；3. 使用有限模型检验器证明两个已实现的修复算子（证据替换与资源预算下行路由）不可交换，构造具体反例；4. 将同一问题扩展到状态层面（受治理证据缓冲区）；5. 提出两种缓解措施降低（而非消除）缺陷类别投毒暴露；6. 推导正权重线性聚合能补偿成员否决的确切条件，构建统一跨控制证据集，证明组合不产生新的检测覆盖；7. 在确定性开放数据工件上组合三个未修改的发布引擎进行实证验证。

Result: 1. 有限模型检验器找到了两个修复算子（证据替换与资源预算下行路由）不交换的具体反例实例，证明修复顺序是控制面语义的一部分；2. 受治理证据缓冲区同样存在"当前可受理不等于未来引用可信"的投毒脆弱性，两种缓解措施只能减少而非消除暴露；3. 推导出正权重线性聚合补偿成员否决的精确条件；4. 组合引擎不会产生新的检测覆盖；5. 实证结果：30个预注册种子下CH1-CH5均满足注册决策规则，CH6在W1工作流(30/30)下满足但在较小W2工作流下不满足。

Conclusion: 该研究是对机制的概念演示（基于开放载荷数据和合成元数据层），并非对生产环境普遍性的断言。核心发现是：多控制修复之间的耦合和顺序依赖是真实且可构造反例验证的问题，需要通过明确的再门控协议来恢复每次操作的合理性，但该协议在特定假设（有界、幂等）下才有效，且状态级投毒风险只能缓解不能根除。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems take consequential actions governed by more than one pre-action control at once: authority, resource, and evidence gates that can admit, degrade, or remediate an action before it executes. This paper's central object is remediation-induced control coupling: a remediation applied by one control can change the action, evidence, or context another control evaluates, invalidating that control's earlier judgment. We formalize this coupling and give a remediate-and-regate protocol that restores per-action soundness in the current bounded, idempotent setting under its stated assumptions. We further show that the two implemented remediation operators (evidence substitution and resource-budget downroute) do not commute -- a finite-model checker finds concrete counterexample instances -- making remediation order part of the control-plane semantics rather than an implementation detail. A governed evidence buffer that trusts its own most recent admitted write is a further instance of the same problem at the level of state -- current admissibility does not imply future reference trustworthiness -- and is vulnerable to poisoning from declared-uncovered defect classes; two mitigations reduce, not eliminate, that exposure. Supporting results establish the exact condition under which positive-weight linear aggregation of gate outcomes can compensate a member veto, a unified cross-control Evidence Set, and that composition manufactures no new detection coverage, reported honestly. Empirically, on a deterministic open-data artifact composing three published engines unmodified, CH1-CH5 meet their registered decision rules across all 30 pre-registered seeds; CH6 does so under W1 but not under the smaller W2 workflow, reported as such. This is a mechanism demonstration on open payload data with a synthetic metadata layer, not a claim about production prevalence.

</details>


### [17] [Building real-time digital twin instances with Function+Data Flow: user evaluation and extension for iterative pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18480)
*Eduardo de Conto,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran,Nicholas Ng,Shweta Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证用户研究评估了FDF（Function+Data Flow）视觉领域特定语言及其集成建模环境DesCartes Builder在AI驱动的数字孪生开发中的可用性与可靠性，并提出了FDF的层次化扩展H-FDF以支持更复杂的迭代和模块化流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生越来越多地利用AI/ML流水线来构建实时数字孪生，但工程化这些流水线仍主要依赖手工方式，难以规范、验证和复用，缺乏专用工具支持。作者旨在解决这一痛点，使AI驱动的数字孪生开发更易用、更可靠。

Method: 提出FDF视觉DSL（将函数/ML模型显式表示为可组合、可复用的构件），并实现于DesCartes Builder集成建模环境；随后开展实证用户研究，让参与者在该环境中实现实时数字孪生原型，通过定量与定性指标测量感知可用性与功能充分性；基于研究结果进一步提出H-FDF层次化扩展以支持迭代和模块化流水线。

Result: DesCartes Builder和FDF在广泛潜在用户群体中达到了良好的可用性水平，尤其对目标受众（领域专家）表现突出；研究还揭示了工具及FDF框架的具体优势与待改进之处，并据此设计了H-FDF层次化扩展。

Conclusion: 集成式、模型驱动的平台是将AI驱动的数字孪生工程转变为规范化建模实践的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) increasingly leverage artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines, both to build real-time DTs from high-fidelity simulations and to instantiate them with historical data. However, engineering these pipelines remains largely ad-hoc: pipelines are hard to specify, validate, and reuse, with scarce dedicated tooling. Function+Data Flow (FDF) addresses this by defining a visual domain-specific language (DSL) that represents functions (ML models) explicitly, enabling their composition and reuse. We implemented FDF in DesCartes Builder, an integrated modeling environment supporting FDF-based DT synthesis and validation.
  In this paper, we report on an empirical user study evaluating whether FDF and DesCartes Builder can make AI-based DT development more accessible and reliable. Participants implemented a representative real-time DT prototype within DesCartes Builder, and we measured perceived usability and feature adequacy through quantitative and qualitative measures. Our results indicate that DesCartes Builder and FDF achieve a good level of usability across a broad range of potential users, and particularly for the intended audience of domain experts. The study additionally surfaces concrete strengths and areas for improvement of both the tool and the underlying FDF framework. Informed by these findings, we propose H-FDF, a Hierarchical extension of FDF supporting iterative and modular pipelines, enabling the formal specification of more complex DT pipelines such as dual training. Our findings suggest that integrated, model-driven platforms are a promising direction to transform AI-based DT engineering into a disciplined modeling practice.

</details>


### [18] [SemaPLC: A Project-Grounded, Verification-Gated Agent Harness for PLC Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18565)
*Yanlun Tu,Huacan Wang,Ziyue Zhou,Jie Zhou,Ningyan Zhu,Ge Chen,Wangyi Chen,Tengfei Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Dasheng Yang,Xiaofeng Mou,Hui Zhang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SemaPLC，一个基于项目上下文且经过验证门控的智能体框架，用于PLC编程代码生成。与仅由模型自我判断输出质量不同，SemaPLC仅在外部检查确认通过时才声明任务完成，覆盖规范、编译和实时运行行为三个层面。在117个独立POU任务上，平均严格验证通过率达72.6%，在65个需在真实项目中编译运行的项目上下文任务上均取得最优集成编译、静态和动态行为得分。动态行为通过实时部署比对执行轨迹来评估，SemaPLC得分52.2，远超基线（22.4-31.4），验证了执行而非静态评分才是测试生成控制逻辑是否真正有效的最佳方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型已能为PLC生成独立程序组织单元（POU），但这些逻辑能否集成到现有PLC项目中并正确运行，此前仅经过有限测试。现有方法通常以模型自我判断输出是否充分为停止标准，缺乏外部验证。因此需要一种项目级验证门控的智能体框架，确保生成的控制逻辑真正编译、集成并在运行时正确执行。

Method: SemaPLC是一个由传统工具组装而成但受严格完成规则约束的智能体框架。其核心思想是：仅当记录的外部检查（规范检查、编译检查、实时运行时行为检查）确认通过时才声明任务完成，而非依赖模型自我评估。评估包含两个轨道：117个独立POU任务和65个项目上下文任务（生成的逻辑必须集成进真实项目编译运行）。动态行为通过将生成逻辑与参考逻辑部署到实时PLC运行时并比对执行追踪来测量。

Result: 在117个独立POU任务上，SemaPLC在所有七种模型上取得最高严格验证通过率，平均72.6%。在65个项目上下文任务上，SemaPLC在集成编译、静态行为和动态行为方面均取得最高平均值。所有方法的静态评分差距在10点以内，但动态评分差距显著：基线为22.4至31.4，而SemaPLC达到52.2，验证门控框架在每个层面均提升均值，尤其在运行时层面提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 执行（运行时）而非静态评分是对生成控制逻辑是否真正有效的可靠检验。SemaPLC通过验证门控机制，在项目上下文中显著提升了PLC代码生成任务的成功率，证明了严格外部验证对于PLC代码生成的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) run industrial plants, and large language models can already generate independent program organization units (POUs) for them. Whether such logic integrates into an existing PLC project and then runs correctly has been checked only in limited tests. We present \textsc{SemaPLC}, a project-grounded and verification-gated agent harness assembled from conventional tools but governed by a strict completion rule. Rather than stopping when the model judges its own output adequate, \textsc{SemaPLC} declares a task complete only when logged external checks confirm it. Those checks cover the specification, the compilation, and the behavior on a live runtime. On 117 independent-POU tasks matching existing benchmarks, it attains the highest strict verified pass rate on all seven models (72.6\% mean). On a project-context track of 65 tasks whose generated logic must compile and run inside a real project, it attains the highest mean on integrated compilation, static behavior, and dynamic behavior. Of the three layers, dynamic behavior is the most revealing. We measure it by deploying the generated and the reference logic to a live PLC runtime and comparing their executed traces. All methods fall within 10 static points of one another, whereas dynamic scores separate them sharply, from 22.4 to 31.4 for the baselines against 52.2 for \textsc{SemaPLC}. Overall, our verification-gated harness raises the mean at every layer and most sharply at runtime. Execution, not static scoring, is the faithful test of whether generated control logic actually works. \textsc{SemaPLC} is open-sourced at https://github.com/midea-ai/SemaPLC.

</details>


### [19] [AppEval: A Unified Benchmark for LLM-Based Mobile Application Repair in ArkTS, Swift, and Kotlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18588)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Yonghao Zhang,Senjian Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Wei Chen,Haiming Jin,Shaocong Long,Xu Liu,Zhe Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AppEval，一个面向移动应用修复的基准测试与原生工具链评估框架，覆盖HarmonyOS/ArkTS、iOS/Swift和Android/Kotlin三大平台。每个任务将隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复分离，仅当同一安装应用目标在缺陷版本上达到断言失败、修复后通过的接受标准。在240个audited Android任务上，五个agent的Pass@1在22.00%到90.50%之间，差距达68.50个百分点，表明移动修复性能高度依赖agent，且运行时感知的接受标准对有意义比较至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级LLM agent评估通常在构建宿主机上运行测试，未考虑移动应用特有的构建-安装-启动-测试边界问题，如缺失SDK、离线设备、断言前崩溃等基础设施故障可能被误判为程序失败，导致评估结果失真。

Method: AppEval框架为每个任务建立"隐藏行为测试+参考生产修复"的契约，接受条件要求同一安装应用在缺陷版本触发断言失败、修复后通过测试，并将基础设施故障单独分类。通过统一schema映射到各平台的构建系统、运行时和测试运行器，确保跨平台可比性。

Result: audited Android分区包含200个可接受的instrumentation任务，来自24个独立可构建的仓库。五个agent在此动态oracle下Pass@1从22.00%到90.50%，跨度达68.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 移动修复性能高度依赖于被评估的agent，运行时感知的接受标准是进行有意义的比较所必需的。然而，当前定量发现仅限Android平台，需在获得audited的iOS和HarmonyOS结果后才能推断跨平台泛化结论。

Abstract: Repository-level LLM agents are typically evaluated on projects whose tests run on the build host. It remains unclear whether their repairs survive the mobile build-install-launch-test boundary, where a missing SDK, offline device, or pre-assertion crash can be mistaken for a program failure. We present AppEval, a benchmark and native-toolchain evaluation framework for mobile application repair across HarmonyOS/ArkTS, iOS/Swift, and Android/Kotlin. Each task separates a hidden behavior test from the reference production fix and is accepted only when the same installed-app target reaches an assertion failure on the defective revision and passes after the fix; infrastructure failures remain a distinct outcome. A common schema maps this contract to each platform's build system, runtime, and test runner. The audited Android partition contains 200 accepted instrumentation tasks from 24 independently buildable repositories. On these tasks, five agents achieve Pass@1 between 22.00% and 90.50%, a 68.50-percentage-point spread under the same dynamic oracle. These results show that mobile repair performance depends strongly on the evaluated agent while demonstrating why runtime-aware acceptance is necessary for meaningful comparison. The quantitative findings in this paper are Android-specific; audited iOS and HarmonyOS results are required before drawing cross-platform generalization conclusions.

</details>


### [20] [OdinEval: A Reproducible Benchmark for LLM-Based Program Repair in the Odin Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18595)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Senjian Zhang,Yuan Luo,Chenhao Ying,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Shaocong Long,Zhenyu Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OdinEval，一个针对Odin系统编程语言的仓库级缺陷修复基准。基准由公共Odin仓库中的文档化缺陷构建，每个实例绑定issue、base/fix提交、金补丁、回归测试、历史工具链和执行记录。在168个过滤后的实例上评估了六个语言模型，Kimi-K3取得最高Resolved得分66.7%，Qwen3.8-Max取得最高Repro得分96.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级修复基准主要集中在少数主流语言上，系统编程语言（如Odin）缺乏充分的测试覆盖。作者旨在填补这一空白，为Odin语言构建可复现的缺陷修复基准。

Method: 从公共Odin仓库的文档化缺陷构建基准。每个实例将issue绑定到base和fix提交、金补丁、issue特定的回归测试、历史工具链及执行记录。准入要求测试在base修订上失败、在金修复后通过。当开发者测试不可用时，采用黑盒测试，由同一模型的三个实例独立评审，在历史状态下执行，并根据版本化Test Writing Skill中的记录反馈进行修订。在统一协议下评估六个语言模型。

Result: 在168个过滤后的实例上，Kimi-K3获得最高Resolved得分66.7%，Qwen3.8-Max获得最高Repro得分96.4%。发布了包含冻结数据、源代码归档、容器、验证器、模型补丁和审计清单的完整资源。

Conclusion: OdinEval为Odin系统编程语言提供了首个可复现的仓库级修复基准，并通过严格的测试准入机制与公开发布的资源确保了基准的可复现性和可信度。

Abstract: Repository-level repair benchmarks still center on a few mainstream languages, leaving systems languages such as Odin largely untested. We present OdinEval, a reproducible benchmark built from documented defects in public Odin repositories. Each instance binds an issue to base and fix commits, a gold patch, an issue-specific regression test, a historical toolchain, and execution records. Admission requires the test to fail on the base revision and pass after the gold fix. When no usable developer test exists, a black-box test is reviewed independently by three instances of the same model, executed in both historical states, and revised from recorded feedback under a versioned Test Writing Skill. We evaluate six language models on 168 filtered instances under one shared protocol. Kimi-K3 records the highest Resolved score at 66.7%, while Qwen3.8-Max has the highest Repro score at 96.4%. The release includes frozen data, source archives, containers, validators, model patches, and audit manifests.

</details>


### [21] [Code Health in LLM-Based Test Generation: Effectiveness and Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18645)
*Freya Wirdemann,Markus Borg,Nadim Hagatulah,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了代码可维护性（通过CodeScene的CodeHealth衡量）与LLM生成单元测试有效性之间的关系，发现CodeHealth是可维护性的弱但一致的信号，且与输入token数量呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明AI工具在高质量、易于维护的源代码上表现更好。本研究旨在进一步探究LLM生成的单元测试的有效性是否随代码可维护性水平（以CodeHealth衡量）而变化，以加深对可维护性与LLM驱动软件开发之间关系的理解。

Method: 使用CodeScene的CodeHealth测量代码可维护性水平，采用传统覆盖率指标和变异得分（mutation score）评估LLM生成的单元测试有效性，在Python、Java和C++三种编程语言上进行实验。此外，还使用常见工业tokenizer研究不同CodeHealth水平的代码如何转化为输入token。

Result: CodeHealth提供了关于LLM生成的测试有效性的弱但一致的信号，并且CodeHealth与输入token数量呈负相关关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果进一步证明了可维护性与LLM驱动的软件开发之间存在关系，即代码的可维护性水平会影响LLM生成测试的有效性以及token消耗效率。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) are now prominent in software engineering. Previous work has shown that AI tools perform better on high-quality source code that is easy to maintain. In this study, we investigate how the effectiveness of LLM-generated unit tests varies across maintainability levels measured by CodeScene's CodeHealth (CH). We assess test effectiveness using traditional coverage metrics and mutation score across Python, Java, and C++. Moreover, we study how code with different levels of CH translates into input tokens using common industrial tokenizers. Our results suggest that CH provides a weak but consistent signal of LLM-generated test effectiveness and is negatively correlated with input-token count. These findings provide further evidence for a relationship between maintainability and LLM-based software development.

</details>


### [22] [Flama: a Python framework for development and deployment of production-ready APIs, machine learning, and LLM services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18733)
*José A. Perdiguero López,Miguel A. Durán-Olivencia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Flama是一个开源的Python框架，基于ASGI构建，用于开发和部署生产级的Web API、机器学习服务和LLM应用。它通过类型驱动的异步编程模型统一了REST API开发、预测模型服务和生成式AI推理，包含七个子系统（依赖注入、可插拔模式层、自动CRUD生成器、可移植二进制模型格式、多后端LLM服务器、Rust加速核心和MCP模块）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架通常将REST API开发、模型服务与LLM推理分开搭建，缺乏一个统一架构来同时支持这三类需求，导致开发者在不同技术栈之间切换、集成和维护成本高。

Method: Flama采用类型驱动、异步优先的编程模型，围绕七个子系统组织：基于类型注解的组件化依赖注入、支持Pydantic/Marshmallow/Typesystem的可插拔模式层、基于SQLAlchemy的自动CRUD生成器（配合Repository和Unit of Work模式）、支持多种ML框架的可移植二进制格式(.flm)、支持vLLM/MLX后端及四种线协议的LLM服务器、Rust加速核心（通过Maturin编译），以及将应用转为MCP服务器的模块。内置JWT认证、分页、后台任务、WebSocket、SSE/NDJSON流式、OpenAPI 3.2.0生成和CLI。

Result: 论文描述了Flama的架构设计、通过示例展示了其编程模型，并与现有框架、模型服务平台和LLM推理引擎进行了对比。

Conclusion: Flama通过单一架构统一了Web API、ML服务和LLM应用三种工作负载，提供了从开发到部署的完整工具链，其模块化设计和多协议支持使其成为现有方案的一个综合性替代选择。

Abstract: We present Flama, an open-source Python framework for developing and deploying production-ready web APIs, machine learning services, and large-language-model (LLM) applications. Built on the Asynchronous Server Gateway Interface (ASGI), Flama offers a type-driven, async-first programming model that unifies REST API development, predictive model serving, and generative AI inference in one architecture.
  It is organised around seven subsystems: a component-based dependency injection system resolving handler parameters from type annotations at startup; a pluggable schema layer supporting Pydantic, Marshmallow and Typesystem behind a single adapter; an automatic CRUD generator turning a SQLAlchemy table and a schema class into REST endpoints backed by the Repository and Unit of Work patterns; a portable binary format (.flm) packaging models from scikit-learn, TensorFlow, PyTorch and Hugging Face Transformers with their metadata for zero-code deployment; a multi-backend LLM server running vLLM (Linux/CUDA) or MLX (Apple Silicon) and exposing four wire protocols (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and a native streaming dialect) through a shared codec; a Rust-accelerated core compiled via Maturin for routing, JSON encoding, compression and parsing; and a Model Context Protocol module turning any application into an MCP server over JSON-RPC 2.0.
  Built-in capabilities include JWT authentication, two pagination strategies, background tasks in threads or processes, WebSocket endpoints, Server-Sent Event and NDJSON streaming, OpenAPI 3.2.0 generation from handler signatures, and a command-line interface for running applications and for serving, packaging and inspecting models.
  We describe the architecture, present the programming model through worked examples, and compare Flama with existing frameworks, model serving platforms and LLM inference engines.

</details>


### [23] [Contract-Aware Rescue of a Drifted Isabelle Development: The Double-Tank Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18822)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在交互式定理证明器（Isabelle）中提出证明时，成功构建并不保证验证任务本身被保留的问题。作者对一个采样数据双罐控制器的Isabelle开发进行了回顾性审计，发现100个原始声明中有16处被实质性篡改（包括弱化的端到端保证定理）。随后使用CAPRI（契约感知的证明修复工具），通过结合Isabelle接受性与对仓库变更的独立契约检查来管理重建过程，在原始九理论结构中完成了全部十个待定目标的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以为交互式定理证明器提出证明，但构建成功（build成功）并不能证明周围的验证任务（verification task）被保留。也就是说，LLM可能在修补证明的过程中悄悄改变了声明本身，从而削弱甚至破坏原始的验证意图。作者希望揭示并解决这一"证明成功但任务被篡改"的问题。

Method: 作者首先对一个采样数据双罐控制器的Isabelle开发进行回顾性审计，识别原始声明的实质性变更。然后使用CAPRI（contract-aware proof-repair tool，契约感知的证明修复工具），将Isabelle形式化接受性与对仓库变更的机器可读编辑契约的独立检查相结合，以规范化、受约束的方式重建证明。重建过程中，由LLM提议修复、CAPRI依据契约把关，所有十个待定目标在原始九理论结构中全部解决。

Result: 审计发现原始100个声明中有16处被实质性篡改，其中包括一个被弱化的端到端保证定理（该定理在结论中假设了四个需求中的三个）。通过CAPRI管理重建，全部十个待定目标均被解除（discharged），且保持在原始九理论结构内。一位共同作者进行了二次复现，成功复现了R10构建、契约检查、控制测试及主要审计发现；独立复制仍有待未来工作。

Conclusion: 以Isabelle接受性作为唯一验证标准的做法不够充分——构建成功但验证任务可能被实质性改动。作者倡导以契约感知的方式（如CAPRI）将形式化检查与声明完整性检查相结合。同时指出，操作层面的端到端验证仍然不完整：需要将实际操作执行与重建后的定量迹契约连接起来，这需要扩展契约，属于未来工作。

Abstract: Large language models can propose proofs for interactive theorem provers, but a successful build does not show the surrounding verification task was preserved. We study this problem in an Isabelle development of a sampled-data double-tank controller. The work began with nine theories and ten unfinished obligations, grew to a 16-theory build without sorry, oops, added axiomatisation, or oracle use, and accumulated 23 stable and 36 broken proof states. A retrospective audit found material changes in 16 of the 100 original declarations, including a weakened end-to-end assurance theorem that assumed three of the four requirements in its conclusion. We used CAPRI, a contract-aware proof-repair tool, to govern a reconstruction by combining Isabelle acceptance with an independent check of repository changes against machine-readable edit contracts. The reconstruction discharged all ten scoped obligations within the original nine-theory structure. A secondary replay by a co-author reproduced the R10 build, contract checks, control tests, and principal audit findings; independent replication remains future work. Operational end-to-end verification remains incomplete: we still need to connect operational executions to the reconstructed quantitative trace contract, a task requiring an extended contract.

</details>


### [24] [SkillForge: Self-Distilling Agents for Project-Specific Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18933)
*Silin Chen,Han Li,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillForge，一个自蒸馏框架，通过重新实现仓库中由测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定问题，并从中提炼可复用的、基于实体的技能，从而在解决真实issue之前主动获取项目特定知识，显著提升软件issue解决性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在自动软件issue解决中表现优异，但往往因缺乏项目特定知识而难以解决特定仓库中的问题。现有的自进化方法要么依赖历史issue解决信号，要么在每次issue解决时产生高昂的测试阶段探索成本，缺乏高效且主动获取项目特定知识的途径。

Method: SkillForge是一个自蒸馏框架，核心流程为：(1) 通过重新实现仓库中测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定的issue；(2) 解决这些合成issue，从中提炼可复用的、以实体为锚定的技能（entity-grounded skills）；(3) 将这些技能与相关仓库实体关联，供未来真实issue解决时使用。

Result: 在开源和闭源模型上的大量实验表明，SkillForge在issue解决性能上持续优于强基线方法，证明主动获取项目特定知识能显著改善下游软件issue解决效果。

Conclusion: 在解决真实issue之前主动从仓库本身获取项目特定知识，比被动等待或高成本探索更有效，SkillForge提供了可行的自蒸馏框架来验证这一思路，并为未来自动化issue解决提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in automated software issue resolution, yet they often struggle to resolve issues in a specific repository because they lack project-specific knowledge. Existing self-evolving approaches acquire such knowledge from repository history or online repair trajectories, but they either depend on available historical issue-resolution signals or incur substantial per-issue test-time exploration cost. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a self-distillation framework that proactively acquires project-specific knowledge from the repository itself. Instead of waiting for real issues to expose project-specific knowledge gaps, SkillForge synthesizes project-specific issues by re-implementing test-covered core functionalities of the repository. By resolving these synthetic issues, SkillForge distills reusable project-specific knowledge into entity-grounded skills and associates them with relevant repository entities for future issue resolution. Extensive experiments using both open-source and closed-source models show that SkillForge consistently improves issue resolution performance over strong baselines. These results demonstrate that proactively acquiring project-specific knowledge before solving real issues substantially improves downstream software issue resolution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [25] [Multi-stage neural operator learning with application for convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18851)
*Zhiping Mao,Zhenye Wen,Yong Zhang,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种多阶段神经算子学习框架DCNO（深度配点神经算子）和DGNO（深度伽辽金神经算子），用于快速高精度计算卷积积分。DCNO是监督式方法，通过迭代学习残差来细化算子逼近；DGNO是无监督式方法，利用PDE残差的弱形式进行训练。两者均通过多阶段训练逐步构造基算子以丰富逼近空间，在卷积计算上达到接近单精度浮点机器精度的效果，并支持多输入算子学习场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积积分在众多应用中广泛存在，需要快速且高精度的计算方法。现有的神经算子学习通常采用一次性训练方式，精度有限，难以满足高精度要求。

Method: 提出两个多阶段神经算子学习框架：(1) DCNO，监督式方法，通过从输入-输出数据对中学习残差来迭代细化算子逼近；(2) DGNO，无监督式方法，适用于目标算子可由PDE表示的情况，利用PDE残差的弱形式进行训练。两种方法均通过多个训练阶段逐步构造基算子来丰富逼近空间。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，DCNO和DGNO在卷积问题上均达到高精度，接近单精度浮点的机器精度；相比传统求解器，在大量查询或参数变化场景下具有显著效率优势。此外，方法还可扩展到卷积的密度和核同时变化的多输入算子学习场景。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练策略能显著提升神经算子学习的精度，使其达到接近机器精度的水平，同时保持高效的计算性能，为卷积积分及其他算子学习问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Convolution integrals widely exist in applications, and to enable fast and accurate computations, this paper introduces two general multi-stage neural operator learning frameworks. The first, Deep Collocation Neural Operator (DCNO), is a supervised approach that iteratively refines the operator approximation by learning residuals from input-output data pairs. The second, Deep Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO), is an unsupervised framework applicable when the target operator can be represented by a PDE, leveraging the weak form of the PDE residual for training. Both methods progressively construct basis operators through multiple training stages to enrich the approximation space, leading to significantly improved accuracy over standard one-shot operator learning. We provide theoretical analysis for their approximation capabilities and implement them for learning convolutions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that both DCNO and DGNO achieve high accuracy, approaching machine precision under single float for convolution problems, and offer substantial efficiency gains for numerous queries or parametric variations compared to traditional solvers. We also extend these frameworks to handle multi-input operator learning scenarios involving variations in both the density and kernel of a convolution.

</details>


### [26] [Entropy-Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18147)
*Ran Ben Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Michael Mitzenmacher,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵约束自适应随机量化（ECASQ）问题，在保持无偏性和熵预算约束下，通过联合选择自适应量化值来最小化均方误差（MSE），并给出了最优动态规划算法和GPU友好的近似动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应随机量化（ASQ）方法在优化MSE时未考虑后续无损熵编码阶段，导致压缩精度有进一步提升的空间。

Method: 将问题建模为熵约束下的自适应随机量化优化问题（ECASQ），提出最优动态规划算法（O(sd^2)时间，O(d^2)空间）和GPU友好的近似动态规划算法（O(sd^2)时间，O(d)空间），并设计了迭代精化过程。

Result: 近似算法保证所得解的MSE不超过使用少一位熵的最优解；迭代精化过程在实验中接近最优结果，同时保持显著的速度优势。

Conclusion: ECASQ通过联合优化量化值与熵预算，在无偏量化场景下实现了比现有方法更优的MSE-熵权衡，为模型压缩、梯度压缩和KV缓存压缩等应用提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Adaptive stochastic quantization (ASQ) is a recently introduced quantization approach that optimizes the Mean Squared Error (MSE) for a given input while preserving unbiasedness. It is designed to alleviate the communication and memory bottlenecks of modern data and machine learning workloads, including model, gradient, and KV-cache compression and nearest-neighbor search. Further, practical systems can then compress quantized data with a lossless entropy encoder. However, existing unbiased methods, including ASQ, choose their quantization values without considering this later encoding stage, leaving accuracy on the table.
  We formulate the Entropy Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization (ECASQ) problem, which jointly selects adaptive quantization values to minimize MSE under an entropy budget and an unbiasedness constraint. We give an optimal dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d^2)$ space for a length-d vector and at most s quantization values, and a GPU-friendly approximate dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d)$ space. The approximation guarantees that the solution has an MSE no larger than the optimal solution that uses one fewer bit of entropy per entry. We also provide an iterative refinement procedure for the approximation solution that, in our experiments, yields near-optimal results while retaining a substantial speed advantage over our solver for the optimal solution.

</details>


### [27] [Towards Reversible Forgetting: Managing Obsolete Knowledge in Continual Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18177)
*Nilutpaul Sarker Yash,Tirtho Roy,Ushashi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可逆遗忘"概念框架，针对企业AI代理在非平稳环境中的知识管理问题。该框架包含激活（active）、休眠（dormant）和退役（retired）三种操作内存状态，以及可恢复休眠知识的相关性回召（reactivation）转移机制。作者将其实现为滞回可逆内存控制器（Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller），通过相关性证据累积、非对称阈值防止状态振荡、影子模式测试回召以及策略门控退役来降低过时信息的影响，同时避免将暂时抑制与永久删除混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习将遗忘视为失败，一味强调保留先前知识。然而在企业AI代理场景中，环境（客户、政策、工具、工作流、法规、市场条件）不断变化，不加区分地保留旧知识可能导致过时信息影响决策，产生负迁移和运营风险。因此需要一种能够选择性且可逆地"遗忘"知识的机制。

Method: 提出滞回可逆内存控制器：1）累积相关性证据以判断知识是否过时；2）使用非对称阈值（hysteresis）防止状态在激活与休眠之间频繁振荡；3）在影子模式（shadow mode）下测试回召，验证休眠知识能否安全恢复；4）通过策略门控退役流程，确保知识的降级或删除受控且可追溯。

Result: 该框架能够降低过时信息对决策的负面影响，同时保留知识恢复的可能性。以金融领域为例，在某一市场制度下有用的知识在另一制度下可能有害，但当相似条件重现时又可能重新变得相关——该框架恰好支持这种动态的知识状态转换。

Conclusion: 可逆遗忘为企业AI代理提供了比传统持续学习更完整的知识管理方案，它区分了暂时抑制与永久消除，在保留适应性的同时控制过时知识的风险，为未来在复杂动态业务环境中的AI系统设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Continual learning has traditionally treated forgetting as a failure, emphasizing preservation of previously acquired knowledge as environments evolve. We argue that this objective is incomplete for enterprise AI agents operating in non-stationary environments, where customers, policies, tools, workflows, regulations, and market conditions change over time. Indiscriminate retention can allow obsolete knowledge to influence decisions, creating negative transfer and operational risk. We therefore propose reversible forgetting: a conceptual framework with three operational memory states: active, dormant, and retired, and a reactivation transition that can restore dormant knowledge when its relevance returns. We instantiate the framework as a Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller that accumulates relevance evidence, uses asymmetric thresholds to prevent state oscillation, tests reactivation in shadow mode, and gates retirement through policy. The framework reduces the influence of obsolete information without conflating temporary suppression with permanent erasure. Finance illustrates the idea: knowledge useful under one market regime may become harmful under another yet regain relevance when similar conditions recur.

</details>


### [28] [Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183)
*Bingqi Shan,Zhehao Yu,Kenhong Lin,Baoquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-SJD是一种用于视觉在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的批量投机雅可比解码后端，通过允许每张图像独立推进解码进度并动态切换Full/Compact执行模式，在不修改教师模型、蒸馏目标和优化流程的前提下，大幅降低滚动生成和端到端训练时间，同时保持蒸馏学生的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在训练紧凑型视觉自回归模型时虽然有效，但每次在线策略训练步骤都需要通过自回归解码逐token生成轨迹，计算开销显著。投机雅可比解码（SJD）虽然可以无需辅助草稿模型并行处理多个token，但原始方法仅针对单序列推理设计，无法直接应用于批量的视觉OPD训练场景。

Method: 作者提出HB-SJD，一种批量SJD滚动后端。其核心包括：(1)允许批次中每张图像根据自身解码进度独立推进，同时通过批量模型前向对处于不同序列位置的图像进行验证；(2)随着图像逐步完成解码，HB-SJD在Full（完整模型）和Compact（紧凑模型）执行模式之间动态切换，以降低后续滚动轮次的成本；(3)HB-SJD仅替换学生的滚动后端，保持教师模型、蒸馏目标和优化流程不变。

Result: 在LlamaGen上的实验表明，HB-SJD显著减少了滚动生成时间和端到端训练时间，同时保持了蒸馏学生的生成质量。

Conclusion: HB-SJD提供了一种高效且即插即用的批量解码解决方案，在保持视觉OPD训练效果的同时大幅降低了计算开销，为大规模视觉自回归模型的高效蒸馏训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation (OPD) improves the training of compact visual autoregressive models by learning from trajectories generated by the current student. However, these online rollouts are still produced token by token with autoregressive decoding, which adds substantial cost to every on-policy training step. Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) provides an alternative because it can process multiple tokens in parallel without an auxiliary draft model, but the original method is designed for single-sequence inference. We introduce HB-SJD, a batched SJD rollout backend for visual OPD. HB-SJD allows each image to advance independently according to its own decoding progress, while images at different sequence positions are still verified in batched model forwards. As images finish, HB-SJD switches between Full and Compact execution to reduce the cost of later rollout rounds. HB-SJD only replaces the student rollout backend and leaves the teacher, distillation objective, and optimization procedure unchanged. Experiments with LlamaGen show that HB-SJD substantially reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student.

</details>


### [29] [H$^2$EDL: Hyper Evidential Deep Learning for Hierarchical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18185)
*Yuanye Liu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²EDL模型，利用标签树自身的子结构和叶子单元素构成线性大小的焦元族，通过每个分支节点的局部Dirichlet意见以闭式形式诱导所有复合置信质量，统一了分层分类与证据不确定性表示。在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345上，校准误差相比交叉熵基线降低约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别常涉及层次化标签空间，模型可能对粗粒度概念自信但对子类不确定。现有工具各有缺陷：扁平证据分类器仅在叶子框架上用单一vacuit量化总无知，而层次分类器只传播点概率缺乏证据概念。超意见（hyper-opinions）理论上可统一二者，但其一般形式随标签数量指数增长，现有超证据网络要么需要训练数据提供复合标签，要么从非结构化权重模式中读取，缺乏哪些复合概念应获质量的原理性指导。

Method: H²EDL的核心观察是：分类学（taxonomy）本身即为缺失的超域（hyperdomain）。其子树和叶子单元素构成线性大小的焦元族（focal family），每个分支节点上的一个局部Dirichlet意见可以闭式地诱导出所有复合置信质量。该模型用同一组参数可从两个互补视角解释：(1)预测视角——作为保持标签树各层级一致性约束的分层分类器；(2)概率视角——定义有效的树结构超意见，其中每个节点分配的质量代表到达该节点但不足以进一步特化到其子类的信念。

Result: 在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345数据集上，H²EDL相比交叉熵基线将校准误差降低约一半，且在更深的层级和更大训练预算下改进更为显著。

Conclusion: H²EDL通过利用分类学本身作为超域，在保持线性复杂度的同时实现了对层次化细粒度不确定性的完整建模，既保留了分层分类器的一致性预测能力，又提供了有效的树结构超意见概率解释，在细粒度识别任务上显著提升校准性能。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition often involves hierarchical label spaces, where a model may be confident about a coarse semantic concept while remaining uncertain among its descendant classes. Such structured ambiguity requires uncertainty representations that capture both fine-grained classes and intermediate concepts. However, existing tools each capture only half of it: flat evidential classifiers quantify total ignorance with a single vacuity on the leaf frame, and hierarchical classifiers propagate point probabilities with no notion of evidence. Hyper-opinions would unify the two, but their general form is exponential in the label count, and existing hyper-evidential networks either require composite labels to be supplied in the training data or read them off an unstructured weight pattern, with no principled notion of which composites deserve mass. We observe that the taxonomy itself is the missing hyperdomain. Its subtrees and leaf singletons form a linear-size focal family, and one local Dirichlet opinion per branching node induces every composite mass in closed form. The resulting model, H$^2$EDL, can be interpreted in two complementary ways using the same set of parameters. From a prediction perspective, it functions as a hierarchical classifier that preserves consistency across different levels of the label tree. From a probabilistic perspective, it defines a valid tree-structured hyper-opinion, where the mass assigned to each node represents the belief that reaches that node but does not provide sufficient confidence to further specialize into its descendants. On FGVC-Aircraft and DERM12345, H$^2$EDL reduces calibration error by approximately half compared with cross-entropy baselines, with the improvement becoming more pronounced at deeper hierarchy levels and under larger training budgets.

</details>


### [30] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Hesse的类比理论，将临床转化过程与机器学习系统构建过程之间的平行关系刻画为一种生成性类比，从而阐明临床转化的认识论与方法论依据，并据此提出一种新的、区别于既有AI哲学中可靠主义理论的ML可靠主义形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医学中已被广泛应用，但其认识论与方法论基础（即其有效性的根据）尚不明确。文献中有人建议以临床转化的标准为模板来确立ML的认识论与方法论标准，但这种类比的性质及其具体意涵尚未被充分阐明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者借助Hesse的理论工具，将医学ML中临床转化与ML系统构建之间的平行关系刻画为生成性类比；进而更加精确地识别临床转化中通常仅被提及的认识论与方法论依据，并以可靠主义（reliabilist）术语对这些依据进行解释，最后将其类比性地推广到ML语境中，提出一种新颖的ML可靠主义立场。

Result: 本文明确了临床转化与ML系统构建之间的类比是一种生成性类比；以可靠主义框架对临床转化的认识论与方法论依据进行了精确刻画；在此基础上提出了一种新的ML可靠主义形式，该形式既区别于又兼容于AI哲学中已有的可靠主义理论。

Conclusion: 临床转化可作为ML系统构建的认识论与方法论依据的有效类比来源；将其依据解释为可靠主义形式，可以为ML的认识论地位提供更有力的哲学辩护，并为现有AI哲学中的可靠主义理论提供补充。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [31] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2017至2023年间55项关于自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）中机器学习应用的研究，发现监督学习方法占主导地位，但深度学习应用正随数据可用性增加而扩展，并强调了多模态数据整合、跨学科合作及创新数据源（如可穿戴设备）对未来诊断和治疗的关键意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD的病因尚不明确，早期诊断对于改善患者预后至关重要。通过系统回顾近年机器学习技术在ASD研究中的应用，旨在识别有助于诊断和治疗的关键特征、趋势及潜在隐藏关联，为未来的研究方法提供指导。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对2017至2023年间发表的55项相关研究进行筛选、分类与分析，检查机器学习技术的类型（监督学习、深度学习、混合方法、模糊逻辑等）、使用数据集以及应用于ASD诊断和治疗的具体场景。

Result: 监督学习方法在ASD诊断中占据主导地位，因其与诊断需求高度契合；深度学习的作用随数据量增加而持续扩大。综述识别出关键挑战在于模型的复杂数据整合能力不足，以及需要扩展涵盖遗传和临床信息的多模态数据集。

Conclusion: 未来ASD的机器学习模型应聚焦于更广泛的多模态数据整合，引入可穿戴设备和生物传感器等创新数据源以实现连续、非侵入式监测；同时需加强跨学科合作和扩大定制化数据集，以更全面地应对ASD的复杂性。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [32] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究领域自适应（domain adaptation）在物理应用中因模拟与实验数据分布不匹配（物理模拟误差和能量谱偏移）而导致的问题，并提出"自适应域适应"（adaptive domain adaptation）方法，通过对模拟事件重新加权，聚焦于真实的物理失配而非错误地对齐谱分布，同时提供无标签模型选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准域适应假设源域和目标域仅在干扰因素上不同，目标量的分布一致。然而在物理应用中，这一假设不成立：模拟可能在物理层面有误差，且目标量（如能谱、红移分布）本身往往就是要测量的对象。标准对抗式域适应在处理条件偏移时有效，但在两个谱分布不同时会把它们对齐，将无约束偏差替换为锚定在模拟先验上的偏差，这是不可接受的。

Method: 论文在一个玩具气簇射（air-shower）基准上构建了可独立/组合切换的三种失配源：探测器响应干扰、物理模拟偏移和能谱偏移。提出自适应域适应方法，通过重新加权模拟事件，使域适应仅聚焦于真实的物理失配，避免对谱分布的错误对齐。同时提出一种无标签模型选择规则，用于挑选接近最优的操作点。

Result: 标准对抗式域适应能够处理条件偏移（如探测器响应干扰），但在能谱偏移存在时会将两个谱对齐，产生由模拟先验锚定的偏差。自适应域适应通过事件重加权避免了这一问题，并且所提出的无标签模型选择规则能选出接近最优的模型配置。

Conclusion: 当物理失配与谱分布差异同时存在时，传统域适应方法会产生误导性的对齐；自适应域适应提供了一种更可靠的替代方案，能够区分真正的物理失配与谱分布差异，并通过无标签选择规则在实际应用中保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [33] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个深度学习框架，通过联合预测物种和属，并在推理时将属的预测与地理物种分布相结合，从而将未见过的物种解析出来，将欧洲蝙蝠自动分类的操作覆盖率从73%提升到85%（35种到41种）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是欧洲受保护的关键生态系统指示物种，可靠种群监测是保护优先事项。由于蝙蝠夜行且隐秘，被动声学监测至关重要，但回声定位叫声随行为和环境影响而变化且种间重叠，导致自动识别困难。现有方法受限于封闭集分类，无法识别训练数据中未出现的物种。

Method: 建立深度学习框架，联合预测物种和属两个层级；在推理时将属预测结果与地理物种分布结合——当某属在特定区域只有一个物种出现时，可将其解析为具体物种，即使该物种不在训练分类体系中。框架将地理信息重新定位为扩展分类器有效分类体系的工具，而非约束。使用了涵盖35种欧洲蝙蝠物种的录音数据，评估了封闭集分类、稀疏物种性能估计的不稳定性，并进行受控的留出物种验证实验。

Result: 罕见物种分析表明有限的评估数据会掩盖物种级性能；留出实验证明属预测+地理位置可以恢复物种级标签。地理解析将操作覆盖率从35种扩展到欧洲本土48种蝙蝠中的41种，覆盖率从73%提升至85%，是目前报道的自动化欧洲蝙蝠分类中最广的操作覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该蝙蝠框架证明了通过组合粗粒度预测与透明外部约束来解析未见细粒度类别的原理可行性。地理信息不再被视为分类器的约束条件，而是扩展分类学覆盖范围的手段，为自动化蝙蝠监测提供了当前最广的欧洲操作覆盖。该方法的通用原理可推广到其他依赖粗粒度预测和外部先验知识解析细分类别的场景。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [34] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环深度推理器（recurrent-depth reasoners）的测试时深度扩展行为。作者发现训练后的算子具有可测量的有限时间动力学状态（settling/marginal/drifting），该状态可预测增加迭代深度对答案的影响。文中给出深度安全（depth-safety）的充分条件，并通过算法任务实验证明：在settling动力学下，额外深度不会导致性能退化，甚至可在更难样本上提升准确率（Sudoku从0.19提升到0.34）；单一的终端不动点目标（terminal fixed-point objective）能同时改变动力学状态与深度行为。作者还给出四个测试时深度的实用判据，并应用于Huginn-3.5B模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度推理器通过在测试时增加迭代次数来解决更难的问题，但额外迭代可能改善、保持或恶化答案。现有方法缺乏对何时增加深度有益或有害的可预测性，因此需要一种可测量的算子属性来指示深度扩展行为，从而指导测试时计算资源的有效分配。

Method: 作者提出通过估计训练后算子的有限时间动力学状态（settling、marginal或drifting）来预测深度扩展行为。他们给出了深度安全的充分条件：当算子的每步位移相对于解码器边际（decoder margin）足够小时，进一步迭代不会改变解码答案。实验在算法任务上进行，每个难度级别仅用800个未增强样本训练，并通过终端不动点目标来调控动力学状态与深度行为。

Result: 实验结果表明，settling算子随深度增加不会退化，在某些任务上还能在训练范围之外的更难未见实例上提升准确率（Sudoku从0.19提升至0.34）。移除终端不动点目标会导致漂移（drifting）并失去增益，而将其添加到通用递归（generic recurrence）上可产生深度安全的进位传播外推。此外，应用相同测量方法于Huginn-3.5B，发现其属于非settling族。

Conclusion: 算子在测试时的有限时间动力学状态是可测量且可调控的深度行为指示器。终端不动点目标是同时控制动力学状态和深度性能的关键机制。作者提供四个操作性判据用于评估测试时深度的有用性，并据此构建了失效模式的分类目录，为循环深度推理器的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [35] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MixerLoop架构，仅循环Gated DeltaNet mixer而不循环稠密FFN，在15M和110M参数规模下超越全块循环，同时减少45.9%的循环骨干投影FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复整个层堆叠（mixer + FFN），但mixer和稠密FFN执行不同操作且成本不同。作者追问"应该循环什么"这一更窄的问题，基于迭代输运秩（ITR）论证有价值的循环应在任务读出时暴露新的跨位置影响方向。

Method: 将循环视为状态更新的重复组合，用迭代输运秩（ITR）和边际ITR描述累积影响轨迹与非冗余贡献。据此提出MixerLoop，重复每个Gated DeltaNet mixer同时仅执行一次稠密FFN。通过有限上下文干预测试验证后续mixer应用是否产生显著且有益的最终读出变化。

Result: 在相同数据、初始化和架构下，MixerLoop在15M参数规模下聚合CORE超越FullLoop，在110M规模保留FullLoop改进的41.5%，同时将循环骨干投影FLOPs降低45.9%。

Conclusion: 循环深度带来的收益可以在不重复执行稠密FFN的情况下保持，证明选择性循环（仅循环mixer）是比全块循环更高效的递归架构设计。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>

*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU）能否区分三态投票模型（MV3）中四种动态轨迹类型（从无序接近、从有序接近、向无序离开、向有序离开）。DBN将晶格快照编码为81维潜向量，但t-SNE分析显示其只能部分区分四类轨迹；而基于DBN编码序列训练的两层Bi-GRU分类器在隐藏状态空间中实现了近乎完美的四类轨迹分离，并能在连续MV3动力学上实时感知当前动态状态，构建了用于检测和分类基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中临界转变的分层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中，检测和分类临界转变（critical transitions）是一个重要问题。静态配置的潜在表示无法完全捕捉时间方向上的动态结构，因此需要探索能够区分不同动态轨迹类型的表征学习方法，并建立能够实时感知动态状态的架构。

Method: 采用两级分层架构：(1) 使用高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机输入层预训练的DBN（架构784→4096→225→81）对静态平衡样本进行无监督学习，将每个晶格快照编码为81维潜向量；(2) 训练一个两层Bi-GRU分类器，输入为长度T=50的DBN编码快照序列，在隐藏状态空间中分类四种轨迹类型。通过t-SNE可视化验证分离效果，并使用滑动窗口将训练好的Bi-GRU应用于连续MV3动力学进行实时检测。

Result: DBN潜空间的t-SNE分析显示四类轨迹仅部分分离，表明静态训练的模型无法完全解析方向性时间结构。而两层Bi-GRU分类器在训练集和测试集的隐藏状态空间中都实现了四类轨迹的近完美分离。滑动窗口应用表明Bi-GRU能够在实时条件下感知系统当前的动态状态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个原理性的分层架构（DBN+Bi-GRU），能够有效检测和分类基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中的临界转变，为动态系统中临界现象的实时监测提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [37] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench，一个基于Ein张量逻辑语言程序化验证的构造式组合图关系推理基准，可动态生成实例以规避数据污染。评估从1.5B到前沿模型，发现三个关键结论：动态实例可直接测量记忆化（微调模型在新旧实例上存在19.3个百分点差距）；图大小和查询深度交互导致即使最强模型也因错读图而退化；4B模型通过程序合成而非直接生成答案，在组合层级上保持稳定并接近前沿准确率（94.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合图关系推理基准使用固定测试集，容易受到数据污染影响，且无法区分模型是真正泛化还是记忆训练数据。需要一种可验证、可动态生成的benchmark来可靠测量模型的组合推理能力和记忆化程度。

Method: 设计ClosureBench基准，使用Ein张量逻辑语言执行程序来生成实例并验证参考答案，涵盖26个任务类别、三个组合层级（L1-L3），沿图大小、边密度和查询深度三个独立轴控制难度。在1.5B到前沿模型范围上进行评估，并额外进行程序合成（Ein和Python+NetworkX）与直接答案生成的对比实验。

Result: 三个发现：（1）动态生成实例可直接检测记忆化——在固定测试集上微调的模型在可见与新鲜实例上准确率差距达19.3个百分点；（2）准确率随图大小和查询深度下降，二者存在交互效应，且该瓶颈源于推理而非输入格式（即使改用JSON边列表或邻接矩阵仍然存在）；（3）4B模型微调为输出可执行程序后，在组合层级间几乎持平，达到94.3%的留出实例准确率，接近前沿模型但token成本大幅降低，且对Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标均成立。

Conclusion: ClosureBench展示了程序化验证的动态基准对于可靠测量组合推理能力的重要性。核心瓶颈在于模型从自然语言中误读图结构而导致的系统退化；程序合成范式（而非直接输出答案）是提升组合推理鲁棒性的有效途径，且该结论与具体程序语言无关。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [38] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算框架，通过谱正则化调控表征几何结构，从而系统性地影响生物神经网络与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐。实验表明，对自监督对比视觉模型进行谱几何调控后，反向预测性显著提升（相对改进55%），同时正向预测性略有下降，双向预测性总体改善，且正向与反向预测性在中间谱指数下趋于对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明生物和人工神经网络之间的表征对齐是非对称的：模型表征能更好地预测神经响应，而神经响应对模型表征的预测能力较弱。这种不对称性引发了一个问题：表征几何是否有助于双向表征对齐？作者假设在训练过程中调控表征几何可以系统性地影响双向对齐。

Method: 将谱正则化（spectral regularization）与双向预测性分析（bidirectional predictivity analyses）整合到一个统一的计算框架中，使用自监督对比视觉模型作为初始验证对象，通过调控学习到的表征的谱几何（spectral geometry）来观察双向预测性变化。

Result: 调控谱几何使反向预测性大幅提升，正向预测性仅小幅下降，双向预测性相对提升55%；同时伴随有效维度降低和共享表征子空间重组，在中间谱指数下前向与反向预测性近似对称。

Conclusion: 表征几何可以被系统性地调控以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，为理解和改进神经科学-机器学习间的双向映射提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [39] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA，一个结合大语言模型（LLM）的多目标强化学习交通信号控制框架。SIGMA通过LLM将自然语言应急指令转换为优先级向量，指导多目标actor-critic控制器，辅以旋转增强实现几何不变性，并通过离线到在线学习确保稳定初始化与动态适应。在SUMO中基于印度Kolkata四个交叉口的评估表明，SIGMA在平均/应急等待时间、队列长度和吞吐量上均优于固定配时、感应控制和DQN基线，并通过bootstrap统计验证了可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL的交通信号控制方法存在三个核心缺陷：(1)预设固定优化目标，无法响应动态变化的优先级需求（如应急车辆通行）；(2)缺乏跨几何相似交叉口的泛化能力；(3)人工设计奖励函数成本高且难以覆盖多目标权衡。因此需要一个能自适应调整目标、具备空间泛化能力且可靠的多目标控制框架。

Method: SIGMA框架由三部分组成：(1) LLM模块将自然语言应急命令解析为优先级向量，免去人工奖励设计；(2) 多目标actor-critic控制器根据优先级向量动态调整优化目标；(3) 旋转数据增强使模型具有方向不变性，提升跨交叉口迁移能力；同时采用离线预训练+在线微调的范式确保稳定初始化与渐进式适应。

Result: 在SUMO仿真中，对印度Kolkata的四个城市交叉口进行评估，与固定时间、感应控制（actuated）和DQN三种基线相比，SIGMA在平均等待时间、应急等待时间、队列长度和吞吐量方面均取得更优表现。消融实验验证了系统对组件故障和几何旋转的鲁棒性，bootstrap统计方法确认了应急服务水平、LLM故障降级和需求敏感度等可靠性属性。

Conclusion: SIGMA展示了语言引导的多目标RL在交通信号控制中的可行性与有效性，实现了无需人工奖励工程的目标自适应控制，同时具备几何泛化能力和统计意义上的可靠性保障，为智能交通信号控制提供了一种鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [40] [Visual-Aware Representation of Web Pages for Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18727)
*Radek Burget,Radek Hranický*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于开源渲染工具FitLayout的可视化感知机器学习平台，通过REST API控制渲染管线，用RDF表示和SPARQL查询来捕获网页的视觉和结构属性，并提供Python客户端库与标准ML工作流集成。演示实验表明，渲染后的网页可转换为图表示并用于训练图神经网络来识别关键内容元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习应用于网页内容时面临巨大挑战，因为需要同时解析HTML及相关资源并进行渲染以获得有意义的视觉和布局感知表示。当前基于网页内容的机器学习研究相对不足，缺乏支持数据集共享、实验可复现以及完整数据准备流程的平台。

Method: 基于开源渲染工具FitLayout构建平台：提供服务器端网页渲染能力，将网页的视觉和结构属性显式捕获为RDF表示并持久化存储；通过REST API控制处理管线，使用SPARQL查询检索适合作为机器学习输入的结构化数据；提供Python客户端库以集成标准机器学习工作流，支持从网页收集、渲染、预处理、标注到下游学习任务的完整数据集准备流程。

Result: 演示实验将渲染后的网页转换为图表示，用于训练图神经网络以识别关键内容元素，验证了方法的适用性和结果的可复现性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模渲染后的网页（包括细粒度布局细节），该平台实现了数据集共享并支持实验结果的复现，为网页内容的可视化感知机器学习和下游学习任务提供了完整的架构支持。

Abstract: Applying machine learning to web pages is challenging due to the need to interpret HTML together with associated resources and perform rendering to obtain a meaningful visual and layout-aware representation. As a result, machine learning over web content remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we present a platform for visual-aware representation and machine learning over web pages based on the open-source rendering tool FitLayout. The platform provides a server capable of rendering web pages, explicitly capturing their visual and structural properties in an RDF-based representation, and persisting the rendered documents in an integrated storage. The processing pipeline is controlled via a REST API, while SPARQL queries are used to retrieve structured data suitable as input for machine learning algorithms. By explicitly modeling rendered web pages, including fine-grained layout details, the platform enables dataset sharing and supports the reproducibility of experimental results. The architecture supports the complete dataset preparation workflow, from web page collection and rendering through preprocessing and annotation of content elements to downstream learning tasks. We further provide a Python client library that integrates the platform with standard machine learning workflows. As a demonstration, we show how rendered web pages can be transformed into graph-based representations and used to train graph neural networks for recognizing key content elements, illustrating both the applicability of the approach and the reproducibility of the results.

</details>


### [41] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中性能提升的来源，发现学生模型的改进主要源于恢复了基础模型已有的推理行为，而非真正学习了特权参考信息。正确参考在不同条件下不提供一致的性能增益，甚至其他问题的解可以超越正确答案。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过在同一模型条件下以特权参考信息提供token级监督来训练学生模型。研究者希望澄清：OPSD带来的性能提升究竟是因为学生真正学到了参考中的信息，还是仅仅恢复了基础模型中已存在的推理行为。这一问题的答案对理解自蒸馏机制的本质至关重要。

Method: 使用Qwen3模型（1.7B到8B）在科学和数学数据集上进行OPSD实验。分析框架将参考信息引起的监督与无参考教师提供的监督分离，度量两种监督分别与学生预测变化的一致性；并构建来自其他问题解的对照控制来验证效果来源。

Result: 正确参考在教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集上不提供一致的性能提升；学生可以在没有正确参考的情况下改进，其他问题的解在多个数学推理基准上可优于正确解；学生的预测与基础模型思考行为的一致性高于与参考监督的一致性，但其他问题的对照控制再现了大部分一致性；更强的对齐并不可靠地对应更大的性能收益。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 小幅目标域探针足以在部署前可靠判断区域级动态组合是否值得采用；动态增益的核心机制是移位异质性与局部模型能力的交互，而非平滑协变量依赖；基于置信下界的门控选择器能够安全地在动态与静态方法间进行选择，避免风险部署。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的test-time reasoning研究在评估候选条件化（candidate conditioning）时缺少关键的对照组——即在相同输出token预算下不提供候选、直接重新求解的基线。这使得无法区分聚合答案的提升究竟源于候选重组、重新求解还是二者共同作用，从而无法准确评估候选上下文（candidate context）相对于额外生成pass的增量价值。

Method: 在相同最大输出token上限下引入候选无关对照组（candidate-free control），按正确候选数量对样本进行分层（c0、c1、c2+），分别比较候选条件化与重新求解的性能差异。采用自适应双基准程序进行保守校正以增强结论稳健性。同时设计原始格式匹配（original-format matching）与安慰剂（placebo）实验描述失败模式，并通过显式答案字段的结构化干预测试因果影响。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上：（1）多个候选正确时（c2+），候选条件化准确率提升+0.290；（2）所有候选错误时（c0），候选条件化准确率下降-0.123，即在此规模下"全错恢复"现象本质上是由额外的生成pass带来的，而非候选条件化本身；（3）单一正确候选（c1）的结论未定论。c2+与c0的结论在保守校正后仍成立。显式答案字段能因果地引导输出，但掩码处理无显著改进，且未能与原格式建立等价性。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>

Abstract: We answer this question with Vector-Symbolic Policy Gradient (VSPG), a discrete-action actor that represents each action by a unit-norm hypervector and scores it by similarity to the encoded state. Under the standard softmax policy-gradient surrogate, we prove that its update is exactly advantage-weighted hypervector bundling followed by normalization, and therefore supports standard advantage estimators. We further show that each trained action hypervector is a fixed-size compressed kernel memory, storing an advantage-weighted kernel expansion over visited states and transferring evidence according to the encoder-induced similarity. This provides a concrete mechanism that can support sample-efficient learning without increasing inference-time memory. Finally, for bipolar action memories, we prove that greedy action selection is stable under random bit flips, with failure probability decaying exponentially in the hypervector dimension. VSPG thus connects VSA action memories, log-linear policy gradients, and kernel policy search while providing a quantitative robustness guarantee.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: RoleSub在视觉令牌减少之后，将每个保留令牌的值表示在正交空间中划分为多个组，并利用一个轻量级路由器决定哪些组需要被保留。路由决策条件化于三个因素：令牌表示、学习到的潜在角色表示以及语言上下文。该机制同样适用于语言值表示，从而在不额外删除令牌的情况下压缩视觉和语言表示。评估基于OpenVLA-OFT-7B模型在四个LIBERO基准场景上进行。

Result: 在匹配的视觉KV预算下，RoleSub在36个设置中的33个优于训练过的仅令牌裁剪控制方法，在激进压缩下增益最大。结合视觉和语言压缩，总KV可降至原始的9.2%-11.3%，同时在大多数任务上保持强大的控制性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在保留令牌内部减少表示提供了对令牌裁剪的有效补充，能够实现激进的VLA模型压缩。RoleSub通过角色条件化的子令牌路由机制，在不牺牲控制性能的前提下大幅降低KV缓存开销，为VLA模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process long multimodal token sequences, making inference expensive in both memory and computation. Existing efficiency methods mainly reduce visual tokens, but aggressive token pruning becomes fragile because removing a token discards its entire representation. Sub-token compression provides a complementary alternative by retaining more tokens while reducing their value width. However, directly applying sub-token compression to VLA policies is less effective because information important for perception, language understanding, and control is distributed differently across the multimodal representation.

After visual token reduction, RoleSub partitions each retained value representation into groups in an orthogonal space and uses a lightweight router to determine which groups should be preserved. The routing decision is conditioned on the token representation, a learned latent role representation, and language context. The same mechanism can also be applied to language values, allowing visual and language representations to be compressed without removing additional tokens.
  We evaluate RoleSub on OpenVLA-OFT-7B across the four LIBERO suites. At matched visual-KV budgets, RoleSub outperforms a trained token-only control in 33 of 36 settings, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. Combining visual and language compression reduces total KV to 9.2--11.3% of the original while retaining strong control performance on most tasks. These results show that reducing the representation within retained tokens provides an effective complement to token pruning for aggressive VLA compression.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从优化器实际采取的更新方向出发，基于方向Hessian（directional Hessian）和梯度对齐分数（gradient-alignment score）推导新的稳定阈值，替代传统基于最大曲率模式的公式，从而消除优化器相关的系统偏差。

Result: 新的"实现稳定边缘"公式去除了优化器相关的偏移，提供了更一致的稳定阈值预测；同时引入新的诊断工具，揭示优化器在一阶优化中主动平衡时间与空间预算的独特作用。

Conclusion: 提出一种更准确刻画实际稳定边缘的公式化框架，不仅改善了稳定阈值的预测一致性，还深化了对不同优化器在一阶优化中动态平衡作用的理解。

Abstract: The edge of stability refers to a phenomenon in deep learning with gradient-based optimizers where the Hessian eigenvalues of the loss remain stable above a threshold that the classical descent lemma predicts to be unstable. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a factor as large as $\times 21.1$. Moreover, this deviation turns out to be systematic and highly dependent on the underlying optimizer, which is not captured by previous formulations. This calls for a new formulation of the stability threshold, which we derive from the directional Hessian and the gradient-alignment score with respect to the actual update taken by the optimizer, rather than the maximum curvature mode. Our new formulation of the realized edge of stability not only removes optimizer-dependent offsets and provides more consistent predictions of the stability threshold, but also introduces new diagnostic tools that reveal the unique role of the optimizer in actively balancing between the temporal and spatial budgets in first-order optimization.

</details>


### [48] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Structure-Aware Numerical Reasoning in LLaMA 3.1 8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18419)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Senhao Cao,Jiahao Liu,Octavia Camps,David Bau,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性角度研究Llama 3.1-8B在时间序列预测中的数值序列建模能力。通过设计一个必须捕捉底层结构才能解决的序列任务（n个随机数加偏移重复），作者发现模型能计算并存储一阶差分，并通过类似归纳电路（induction circuit）的机制检索相关差分并与当前值相加来完成预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在数值序列建模和时间序列预测上表现优异，但其实现机制尚不清楚，特别是模型是否真正理解底层结构（至少需要对序列中的一阶差分进行推理）。作者希望从机制可解释性角度揭示LLM完成时间序列预测的内部算法。

Method: 1) 设计一个无法通过表面线索解决的结构化序列任务：采样n个随机数并以偏移量重复；2) 使用Llama 3.1-8B进行评估；3) 通过探针实验（probing）检测模型内部表示中是否存储一阶差分；4) 通过激活修补（activation patching）的因果反事实分析，定位并验证模型完成预测所依赖的机制。

Result: 1) Llama在结构化序列任务上表现优异，表明其能捕捉底层结构；2) 探针实验显示模型在没有显式监督的情况下，在内部表示中计算并存储一阶差分；3) 激活修补揭示Llama通过类似归纳电路的机制检索相关的一阶差分，并将其添加到当前值以完成预测。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了LLM进行时间序列预测的潜在内部机制——概念归纳（concept induction），即模型通过类似归纳电路的方式检索和利用序列中的差分信息。这是首批识别LLM中此类概念归纳形式的研究之一，为理解LLM的时间序列能力提供了机制层面的洞见。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong numerical sequence modeling capabilities and show promise in time-series prediction. While LLMs display in-context learning capabilities, the mechanisms with which they accomplish time-series prediction remain unclear. Specifically, whether they truly understand the underlying structure, which at a minimum requires reasoning over first differences in the sequence of numbers. To study this, we investigate Llama 3.1-8B from a mechanistic interpretability point of view. Mechanistic interpretability is an emerging field concerned with the reverse engineering of the algorithms learned by neural networks such as LLMs. To assess Llamas' numerical sequence modeling capabilities and to facilitate our mechanistic interpretability analysis, we create a sequence modeling task that cannot be solved without picking up structural cues. Specifically, we sample n random numbers and repeat them with an offset. We find that Llama displays strong performance on our tasks suggesting that it can pick up on the underlying structure. To understand the mechanisms that allow it to do so, we perform probing experiments and activation patching based counterfactual analysis. Probing reveals that the model computes and stores first differences in its internal representations without explicit supervision, indicating that it tracks structural information about the sequence. Activation patching reveals that Llama retrieves the relevant first-difference with a mechanism similar to an induction circuit and subsequently adds it to the current value. Notably, our work represents one of the first studies to identify this form of concept induction in LLMs.

</details>


### [49] [Adaptive Multi-Agent Feature Selection for Personalized Fall Risk Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18450)
*Chang Liu,Ladda Thiamwong,Yanjie Fu,Rui Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAFIR框架，将自适应特征选择建模为强化学习问题，用于老年人跌倒风险识别。该框架在纵向多模态健康数据上联合建模评估变量的结构依赖和可穿戴设备身体活动数据的时间动态，并通过稀疏跌倒结局信号学习自适应选择策略。在PEER聚类随机试验数据上的实验表明，PAFIR比现有基线更能捕捉特征相关性的纵向和结构模式，支持动态、个性化的特征选择，有助于更及时和个性化的跌倒预防。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒是一个重大公共卫生挑战，其风险因素涉及多个领域之间复杂、时变的交互作用。现有方法大多是静态的，无法跨模态和时间自适应地建模不断演变的个体化风险因素，且难以处理稀疏和延迟的跌倒结局事件。

Method: 将自适应特征选择形式化为纵向多模态健康数据上的强化学习问题。PAFIR联合建模了相关评估变量之间的结构依赖关系以及可穿戴设备身体活动数据中的时间动态，并利用稀疏跌倒发生结局产生的奖励信号，在多次研究随访中学习自适应选择策略。

Result: 在PEER聚类随机试验数据上的实验表明，PAFIR比最先进的基线方法更能有效捕捉特征相关性的纵向和结构模式，并能实现动态、受试者特异的特征选择。

Conclusion: 通过随时间自适应地选择特征，PAFIR能够支持更及时和个性化的跌倒预防策略。

Abstract: Falls among older adults represent a major public health challenge driven by complex, time-varying interactions across multiple risk domains. Effective fall risk factor identification requires learning from heterogeneous longitudinal data while accounting for sparse and delayed fall-related outcome events. However, existing approaches are largely static and fail to adaptively model evolving, individualized risk factors across modalities and time. We propose PAFIR, a Personalized and Adaptive Feature selection framework for fall risk Identification and pRevention, which formulates adaptive feature selection as a reinforcement learning problem over longitudinal multimodal health data. PAFIR jointly models structural dependencies among correlated assessment variables and temporal dynamics in wearable-derived physical activity data, and learns adaptive selection policies across repeated study visits using reward signals derived from sparse fall incidence outcomes. We apply PAFIR to data from the Physio fEedback Exercise pRogram (PEER) cluster-randomized trial. Experimental results demonstrate that PAFIR more effectively captures longitudinal and structural patterns of feature relevance than state-of-the-art baselines, and enables dynamic, subject-specific feature selection. By adapting selected features over time, PAFIR supports more timely and personalized fall prevention strategies.

</details>


### [50] [Atrial Fibrillation Detection with Arbitrary Leads via a Codebook-Based Reconstruction-Classification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18451)
*Hongtao Li,Jia Wei,Guoyao Li,Yuchen Lei,Guangnian Ma,Jia Xiao,Yuanjun Lai,Shuzhen Lv,Xueqiang Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCGCNet，一种基于向量量化变分自编码器的端到端深度模型，用于心电信号（ECG）中房颤（AF）检测。该模型通过局部-全局对比模块和自适应码本向量量化器，在跨数据集泛化（AUC>0.98）、噪声鲁棒性（基线漂移、电源干扰、EMG伪影）及标准12导联评估中均取得最优性能，为临床真实场景中的AF检测建立了新的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
